FOE Calendar

Forecast of Events calendar spike. Dummy data, paste-ready screens.

Guide library What this is Before you start Step 1 - App.OnStart (dummy data) Step 2 - Stage 1: calendar, dashboard, filters Step 3 - Stage 2: day pop-up and event detail Step 4 - Stage 3: add, edit, delete and roles Step 5 - Admin screen and the final calendar How to report back What is being tested, and how it works
Forecast of Events Dummy data only v1 · 16/11/2026 08:14 GMT

FOE Calendar - spike

One calendar screen and one admin screen, running entirely on dummy data held inside the app. Nothing is connected to a list and nothing is saved. The point is to find out, in real Power Apps, what works and what does not.

What this is

This is the "art of the possible" test for the Forecast of Events (FOE) calendar. It is built from the design concept pack, using real event titles from the National FOE spreadsheet but with dates moved to sit around today (about six weeks back to five months ahead), so the calendar always has something in it. The requirement and attendance details on 8 events are invented samples.

Nothing here is stored. Add, edit, delete and admin changes change in-app collections only. Close and reopen the app and everything resets.

What you can test

  • Month calendar with event chips, a "+N more" link, today highlighted, and month arrows.
  • Five dashboard cards (the "Resources to organise" count uses the 60-day warning rule).
  • Type chips, a status filter, whole-programme search, and Reset filters.
  • Click a day for a list, click an event for its detail (People, Money, Equipment, branch attendance).
  • Add, edit and delete events (delete moves to a "deleted" flag), with validation messages.
  • A Viewing as button that cycles Viewer, Editor and Admin.
  • An Admin screen: event types (rename, recolour, show or hide, add), requirement settings, deleted events (restore) and roles.

What it leaves out on purpose

Home tiles and programme tabs, week and agenda views, the Meetings tab, recurrence, a real people picker, and every SharePoint connection. They come after we know the core works.

Before you start

  1. Make a new blank canvas app, called FOE Calendar Spike. Use the Tablet layout (landscape), and leave Scale to fit on (the default). 1366 x 768 works, and a taller screen (for example 1920 x 1080) lets each day show three events instead of two, because the number of chips depends on the cell height.
  2. Check Settings → Updates and make sure Modern controls and themes is on. The screens use modern text boxes, dropdowns, date pickers and buttons.
  3. Do not add any data connections. There are none to add.
  4. Keep the first blank screen for now. You will paste the calendar into a new screen and delete the blank one.
Each stage file is a complete replacement for the one before. To move to the next stage, paste the new file, then delete the older scrCalendar screen and rename the new one back to scrCalendar if Studio added a number to the end.

1App.OnStart (dummy data)

  1. Select the App object (top of the Tree view) → OnStart property.
  2. Select all, delete, and paste the block below. It stays as App.OnStart. It is never part of a screen.
  3. Run it: in the Tree view, right-click App → Run OnStart.
App - OnStart (dummy data, 81 events) v1 · 16/11/2026 08:14 GMT

ClearCollect(
    colEvents,
    ForAll(
        colBase As b,
        {
            Id: b.I,
            Title: b.T,
            StartDate: DateAdd(Today(), b.O, "Days"),
            EndDate: DateAdd(Today(), b.O + b.L - 1, "Days"),
            TypeKey: b.K,
            Status: If(b.S = "T", "To be confirmed", "Confirmed"),
            Kind: Coalesce(b.Kd, "event"),
            KeyDate: Coalesce(b.Kf, false),
            Overseas: Coalesce(b.Ov, false),
            Programme: Coalesce(b.Pg, "National Activity"),
            PeopleState: Coalesce(b.P, ""),
            PeopleNote: Coalesce(b.Pn, ""),
            MoneyState: Coalesce(b.M, ""),
            MoneyNote: Coalesce(b.Mn, ""),
            EquipState: Coalesce(b.E, ""),
            EquipNote: Coalesce(b.En, ""),
            BranchReq: Coalesce(b.B, false),
            BranchWho: Coalesce(b.W, ""),
            Location: "",
            Organiser: "",
            Notes: "",
            Sample: !IsBlank(b.P),
            Deleted: false,
            SortKey: b.O * 1000 - b.L
        }
    )
);
ClearCollect(colSelTypes, ForAll(Filter(colTypes, Active), {TKey: ThisRecord.TKey}));
ClearCollect(
    colVisible,
    AddColumns(
        SortByColumns(
            Filter(
                colEvents,
                !Deleted &&
                (TypeKey in colSelTypes.TKey) &&
                (varStatus = "All" || Status = varStatus) &&
                (IsBlank(varSearch) || Lower(varSearch) in Lower(Title))
            ),
            "SortKey",
            SortOrder.Ascending
        ),
        Concern,
        !Deleted && EndDate >= Today() && DateDiff(Today(), StartDate, "Days") <= varThresh && (PeopleState = "needs" || MoneyState = "needs" || EquipState = "needs"),
        Hex,
        With({k: TypeKey}, LookUp(colTypes, TKey = k).Hex)
    )
);
ClearCollect(
    colDays,
    ForAll(
        Sequence(42) As s,
        With(
            {d: DateAdd(varGridStart, s.Value - 1, "Days")},
            With(
                {f: Filter(colVisible, StartDate <= d && EndDate >= d)},
                With(
                    {e1: If(CountRows(f) >= 1, Index(f, 1)), e2: If(CountRows(f) >= 2, Index(f, 2)), e3: If(CountRows(f) >= 3, Index(f, 3))},
                    {
                        Idx: s.Value,
                        D: d,
                        N: CountRows(f),
                        T1: If(IsBlank(e1), "", If(e1.Concern, "! ", "") & e1.Title),
                        F1: If(IsBlank(e1), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0), ColorFade(ColorValue(e1.Hex), 0.8)),
                        B1: If(IsBlank(e1), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0), If(e1.Concern, RGBA(142, 21, 55, 1), If(e1.Status = "To be confirmed", ColorValue(e1.Hex), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0)))),
                        W1: If(IsBlank(e1), 0, If(e1.Concern, 2, If(e1.Status = "To be confirmed", 1, 0))),
                        S1: !IsBlank(e1) && e1.Status = "To be confirmed" && !e1.Concern,
                        I1: If(IsBlank(e1), 0, e1.Id),
                        T2: If(IsBlank(e2), "", If(e2.Concern, "! ", "") & e2.Title),
                        F2: If(IsBlank(e2), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0), ColorFade(ColorValue(e2.Hex), 0.8)),
                        B2: If(IsBlank(e2), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0), If(e2.Concern, RGBA(142, 21, 55, 1), If(e2.Status = "To be confirmed", ColorValue(e2.Hex), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0)))),
                        W2: If(IsBlank(e2), 0, If(e2.Concern, 2, If(e2.Status = "To be confirmed", 1, 0))),
                        S2: !IsBlank(e2) && e2.Status = "To be confirmed" && !e2.Concern,
                        I2: If(IsBlank(e2), 0, e2.Id),
                        T3: If(IsBlank(e3), "", If(e3.Concern, "! ", "") & e3.Title),
                        F3: If(IsBlank(e3), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0), ColorFade(ColorValue(e3.Hex), 0.8)),
                        B3: If(IsBlank(e3), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0), If(e3.Concern, RGBA(142, 21, 55, 1), If(e3.Status = "To be confirmed", ColorValue(e3.Hex), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0)))),
                        W3: If(IsBlank(e3), 0, If(e3.Concern, 2, If(e3.Status = "To be confirmed", 1, 0))),
                        S3: !IsBlank(e3) && e3.Status = "To be confirmed" && !e3.Concern,
                        I3: If(IsBlank(e3), 0, e3.Id)
                    }
                )
            )
        )
    )
);
ClearCollect(colList, Filter(colVisible, false))
Check: in View → Collections you should see colEvents (81 rows), colTypes (16), colVisible, colDays (42) and several small option tables. If a collection is missing, OnStart hit an error: tell me the message.

2Stage 1 - calendar, dashboard, filters

In the Tree view, add a new blank screen, then select it and paste the YAML (the same paste you use on the other guides). If Studio asks, the screen will arrive named scrCalendar.

Screen - scrCalendar (Stage 1) v1 · 16/11/2026 08:14 GMT
Show the code (1,276 lines) 
Screens:

    Properties:
      Fill: =RGBA(247, 244, 246, 1)

            X: =0
            Y: =0
          Children:
            - conBand:
                Control: GroupContainer@1.5.0
                Variant: ManualLayout

                  X: =0
                  Y: =0
                Children:
                  - lblTitle:
                      Control: ModernText@1.0.0
                      Properties:
                        Color: =RGBA(255, 255, 255, 1)

                      Control: Classic/Button@2.2.0
                      Properties:

                        RadiusBottomLeft: =14

                Control: GroupContainer@1.5.0
                Variant: ManualLayout

                  DropShadow: =DropShadow.None

Test checklist

  • The purple band, five dashboard cards, toolbar, type chips, Mon to Sun header and a 6-row month grid appear with no red error markers in the Tree view.
  • Today's date has a purple circle. Days outside the month are greyed.
  • Days with events show coloured chips. Days with more show "+N more".
  • A chip with a dashed outline is "To be confirmed". A maroon outline with a leading ! means the event is close and something is still to organise.
  • The month arrows and Today change the month. The chips change with it.
  • Clicking a type chip shows only that type. Clicking more adds them. Clicking the only selected chip shows all again.
  • The status dropdown filters. Typing in search and clicking away (or pressing Tab) filters. Reset filters clears all three and appears only when something is filtered.
  • Clicking a day or an event shows a notification saying Stage 2 adds the pop-ups.
  • The Viewing as button cycles Viewer, Editor, Admin.

Test checklist

  • Click a day: a list pop-up opens with that day's events, each with P, M, E markers and a "Branch" tag where needed.
  • Click a row (or a chip, or the Next event card): the detail pop-up opens with type, status, dates, the three resource rows, branch attendance and details.
  • Open D&I Conf 1 (about a week ahead): it shows the amber "Needs attention" banner.
  • Back to list appears only when you came from a list. Close and the dark background both close the pop-up.
  • The "Starting in 30 days", "Still to be confirmed" and "Resources to organise" cards open lists.

                        FontWeight: =FontWeight.Semibold
                        Height: =34
                        HoverColor: =RGBA(255, 255, 255, 1)
                        HoverFill: =RGBA(83, 42, 69, 1)
                        OnSelect: |-
                          =If(
                              IsBlank(Trim(txtFTitle.Text)),
                              Set(varFormErr, "Enter an event title."),
                              IsBlank(dpkFStart.SelectedDate) || IsBlank(dpkFEnd.SelectedDate),
                              Set(varFormErr, "Choose a start date and an end date."),
                              dpkFEnd.SelectedDate < dpkFStart.SelectedDate,
                              Set(varFormErr, "The end date cannot be before the start date."),
                              tglFBranch.Value && IsBlank(Trim(txtFWho.Text)),
                              Set(varFormErr, "Branch attendance is required: say who is going, or untick the box."),
                              Set(varFormErr, "");
                              If(
                                  varFormMode = "edit",
                                  Patch(
                                      colEvents,
                                      LookUp(colEvents, Id = varEvent.Id),
                                      {
                                      Title: Trim(txtFTitle.Text),
                                      StartDate: dpkFStart.SelectedDate,
                                      EndDate: dpkFEnd.SelectedDate,
                                      TypeKey: drpFType.Selected.TKey,
                                      Status: drpFStatus.Selected.Value,
                                      Kind: drpFKind.Selected.Value,
                                      KeyDate: tglFKey.Value,
                                      Overseas: tglFOv.Value,
                                      Programme: drpFProg.Selected.Value,
                                      PeopleState: drpFP.Selected.Code,
                                      PeopleNote: Trim(txtFPn.Text),
                                      MoneyState: drpFM.Selected.Code,
                                      MoneyNote: Trim(txtFMn.Text),
                                      EquipState: drpFE.Selected.Code,
                                      EquipNote: Trim(txtFEn.Text),
                                      BranchReq: tglFBranch.Value,
                                      BranchWho: Trim(txtFWho.Text),
                                      Location: Trim(txtFLoc.Text),
                                      Organiser: Trim(txtFOrg.Text),
                                      Notes: Trim(txtFNotes.Text),
                                      SortKey: DateDiff(Today(), dpkFStart.SelectedDate, "Days") * 1000 - (DateDiff(dpkFStart.SelectedDate, dpkFEnd.SelectedDate, "Days") + 1)
                                      }
                                  );
                                  Set(varEvent, LookUp(colEvents, Id = varEvent.Id)),
                                  Set(varNewId, Coalesce(Max(colEvents, Id), 0) + 1);
                                  Collect(
                                      colEvents,
                                      {
                                      Id: varNewId,
                                      Sample: false,
                                      Deleted: false,
                                      Title: Trim(txtFTitle.Text),
                                      StartDate: dpkFStart.SelectedDate,
                                      EndDate: dpkFEnd.SelectedDate,
                                      TypeKey: drpFType.Selected.TKey,
                                      Status: drpFStatus.Selected.Value,
                                      Kind: drpFKind.Selected.Value,
                                      KeyDate: tglFKey.Value,
                                      Overseas: tglFOv.Value,
                                      Programme: drpFProg.Selected.Value,
                                      PeopleState: drpFP.Selected.Code,
                                      PeopleNote: Trim(txtFPn.Text),
                                      MoneyState: drpFM.Selected.Code,
                                      MoneyNote: Trim(txtFMn.Text),
                                      EquipState: drpFE.Selected.Code,
                                      EquipNote: Trim(txtFEn.Text),
                                      BranchReq: tglFBranch.Value,
                                      BranchWho: Trim(txtFWho.Text),
                                      Location: Trim(txtFLoc.Text),
                                      Organiser: Trim(txtFOrg.Text),
                                      Notes: Trim(txtFNotes.Text),
                                      SortKey: DateDiff(Today(), dpkFStart.SelectedDate, "Days") * 1000 - (DateDiff(dpkFStart.SelectedDate, dpkFEnd.SelectedDate, "Days") + 1)
                                      }
                                  )
                              );
                              ClearCollect(
                                  colVisible,
                                  AddColumns(
                                      SortByColumns(
                                          Filter(
                                              colEvents,
                                              !Deleted &&
                                              (TypeKey in colSelTypes.TKey) &&
                                              (varStatus = "All" || Status = varStatus) &&
                                              (IsBlank(varSearch) || Lower(varSearch) in Lower(Title))
                                          ),
                                          "SortKey",
                                          SortOrder.Ascending
                                      ),
                                      Concern,
                                      !Deleted && EndDate >= Today() && DateDiff(Today(), StartDate, "Days") <= varThresh && (PeopleState = "needs" || MoneyState = "needs" || EquipState = "needs"),
                                      Hex,
                                      With({k: TypeKey}, LookUp(colTypes, TKey = k).Hex)
                                  )
                              );
                              ClearCollect(
                                  colDays,
                                  ForAll(
                                      Sequence(42) As s,
                                      With(
                                          {d: DateAdd(varGridStart, s.Value - 1, "Days")},
                                          With(
                                              {f: Filter(colVisible, StartDate <= d && EndDate >= d)},
                                              With(
                                                  {e1: If(CountRows(f) >= 1, Index(f, 1)), e2: If(CountRows(f) >= 2, Index(f, 2)), e3: If(CountRows(f) >= 3, Index(f, 3))},
                                                  {
                                                      Idx: s.Value,
                                                      D: d,
                                                      N: CountRows(f),
                                                      T1: If(IsBlank(e1), "", If(e1.Concern, "! ", "") & e1.Title),
                                                      F1: If(IsBlank(e1), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0), ColorFade(ColorValue(e1.Hex), 0.8)),
                                                      B1: If(IsBlank(e1), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0), If(e1.Concern, RGBA(142, 21, 55, 1), If(e1.Status = "To be confirmed", ColorValue(e1.Hex), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0)))),
                                                      W1: If(IsBlank(e1), 0, If(e1.Concern, 2, If(e1.Status = "To be confirmed", 1, 0))),
                                                      S1: !IsBlank(e1) && e1.Status = "To be confirmed" && !e1.Concern,
                                                      I1: If(IsBlank(e1), 0, e1.Id),
                                                      T2: If(IsBlank(e2), "", If(e2.Concern, "! ", "") & e2.Title),
                                                      F2: If(IsBlank(e2), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0), ColorFade(ColorValue(e2.Hex), 0.8)),
                                                      B2: If(IsBlank(e2), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0), If(e2.Concern, RGBA(142, 21, 55, 1), If(e2.Status = "To be confirmed", ColorValue(e2.Hex), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0)))),
                                                      W2: If(IsBlank(e2), 0, If(e2.Concern, 2, If(e2.Status = "To be confirmed", 1, 0))),
                                                      S2: !IsBlank(e2) && e2.Status = "To be confirmed" && !e2.Concern,
                                                      I2: If(IsBlank(e2), 0, e2.Id),
                                                      T3: If(IsBlank(e3), "", If(e3.Concern, "! ", "") & e3.Title),
                                                      F3: If(IsBlank(e3), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0), ColorFade(ColorValue(e3.Hex), 0.8)),
                                                      B3: If(IsBlank(e3), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0), If(e3.Concern, RGBA(142, 21, 55, 1), If(e3.Status = "To be confirmed", ColorValue(e3.Hex), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0)))),
                                                      W3: If(IsBlank(e3), 0, If(e3.Concern, 2, If(e3.Status = "To be confirmed", 1, 0))),
                                                      S3: !IsBlank(e3) && e3.Status = "To be confirmed" && !e3.Concern,
                                                      I3: If(IsBlank(e3), 0, e3.Id)
                                                  }
                                              )
                                          )
                                      )
                                  )
                              );
                              Notify(If(varFormMode = "edit", "Changes saved", "Event added") & " (dummy data only: not stored).", NotificationType.Success);
                              Set(varDlg, If(varFormMode = "edit", "detail", ""))
                          )

Test checklist

  • As Viewer: no Add event button and no Edit or Delete in the detail. As Editor or Admin: they appear.
  • + Add event opens the form. Try Save with an empty title, an end date before the start date, and Branch attendance ticked with no name. Each gives a message at the bottom.
  • Save a valid event. It appears on the calendar straight away, and the dashboard counts move.
  • Open an event, choose Edit: the form is pre-filled. Change something and save. The detail updates.
  • Delete asks first, then the event disappears from the calendar.
  • After using the form, open Add event again: the fields are back to their defaults (this tests Reset()).

5Admin screen and the final calendar

  1. Add another blank screen and paste scrAdmin below.
  2. Then paste the final calendar (it is Stage 3 plus an "Admin area" button, visible to Admins only). Replace Stage 3 as before.

                                ClearCollect(
                                    colVisible,
                                    AddColumns(
                                        SortByColumns(
                                            Filter(
                                                colEvents,
                                                !Deleted &&
                                                (TypeKey in colSelTypes.TKey) &&
                                                (varStatus = "All" || Status = varStatus) &&
                                                (IsBlank(varSearch) || Lower(varSearch) in Lower(Title))
                                            ),
                                            "SortKey",
                                            SortOrder.Ascending
                                        ),
                                        Concern,
                                        !Deleted && EndDate >= Today() && DateDiff(Today(), StartDate, "Days") <= varThresh && (PeopleState = "needs" || MoneyState = "needs" || EquipState = "needs"),
                                        Hex,
                                        With({k: TypeKey}, LookUp(colTypes, TKey = k).Hex)
                                    )
                                );
                                ClearCollect(
                                    colDays,
                                    ForAll(
                                        Sequence(42) As s,
                                        With(
                                            {d: DateAdd(varGridStart, s.Value - 1, "Days")},
                                            With(
                                                {f: Filter(colVisible, StartDate <= d && EndDate >= d)},
                                                With(
                                                    {e1: If(CountRows(f) >= 1, Index(f, 1)), e2: If(CountRows(f) >= 2, Index(f, 2)), e3: If(CountRows(f) >= 3, Index(f, 3))},
                                                    {
                                                        Idx: s.Value,
                                                        D: d,
                                                        N: CountRows(f),
                                                        T1: If(IsBlank(e1), "", If(e1.Concern, "! ", "") & e1.Title),
                                                        F1: If(IsBlank(e1), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0), ColorFade(ColorValue(e1.Hex), 0.8)),
                                                        B1: If(IsBlank(e1), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0), If(e1.Concern, RGBA(142, 21, 55, 1), If(e1.Status = "To be confirmed", ColorValue(e1.Hex), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0)))),
                                                        W1: If(IsBlank(e1), 0, If(e1.Concern, 2, If(e1.Status = "To be confirmed", 1, 0))),
                                                        S1: !IsBlank(e1) && e1.Status = "To be confirmed" && !e1.Concern,
                                                        I1: If(IsBlank(e1), 0, e1.Id),
                                                        T2: If(IsBlank(e2), "", If(e2.Concern, "! ", "") & e2.Title),
                                                        F2: If(IsBlank(e2), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0), ColorFade(ColorValue(e2.Hex), 0.8)),
                                                        B2: If(IsBlank(e2), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0), If(e2.Concern, RGBA(142, 21, 55, 1), If(e2.Status = "To be confirmed", ColorValue(e2.Hex), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0)))),
                                                        W2: If(IsBlank(e2), 0, If(e2.Concern, 2, If(e2.Status = "To be confirmed", 1, 0))),
                                                        S2: !IsBlank(e2) && e2.Status = "To be confirmed" && !e2.Concern,
                                                        I2: If(IsBlank(e2), 0, e2.Id),
                                                        T3: If(IsBlank(e3), "", If(e3.Concern, "! ", "") & e3.Title),
                                                        F3: If(IsBlank(e3), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0), ColorFade(ColorValue(e3.Hex), 0.8)),

Test checklist

  • Set Viewing as to Admin. An Admin area button appears. It opens the admin screen.
  • Event types: rename one (click away to save), cycle its colour, hide it, add a new one. Go back to the calendar: chips and forms reflect it.
  • Requirements and alerts: change the warning days (try 5), and the "Resources to organise" count changes. Rename the state wording and see it in the detail pop-up.
  • Deleted events: delete an event on the calendar, then restore it here.
  • People and access: reads as three role descriptions.

How to report back

For anything that does not work, tell me which stage and:

  • the exact error text, and which control it is on (Tree view, the red cross, or the formula bar message);
  • or a screenshot of what you see against what the guide says you should see;
  • what you clicked, and what happened instead.

It is fine, and useful, for things not to work. Each failure tells us what the real limits are.

If Studio flags a property

The YAML passed Microsoft's parser and the structure checks. A few properties could not be proven against your earlier apps, though Microsoft's documentation lists them. If Studio reports PA2108 (unknown property) on one of these, tell me which and delete just that line to carry on:

  • Align or BorderStyle on a Classic Button (the event chips);
  • WrapCount on galDays (the month grid);
  • OnChange or Default on a modern text input.

What is being tested, and how it works

The month grid

The grid is one gallery, galDays, with 42 items and WrapCount = 7. Each item is one day, with a single cell template. This is far smaller than hand-placing 42 cells, and it tests whether a wrapped gallery gives a clean calendar. If it misbehaves (cells not seven across, wrong heights), I will switch to hand-placed cells or the Calendar Pro week-row approach.

Where the data comes from

NameWhat it holds
colEventsAll dummy events (81). Add, edit, delete and restore change this.
colVisibleThe events that pass the search, type and status filters, with a Concern flag and a colour. Rebuilt whenever a filter changes.
colDays42 rows, one per cell in the grid: the date, the number of events and the text, colour and border for the first three. Rebuilt whenever the filters or the month change. This is what keeps each cell's formulas short.
colTypesThe event types and their colours (edited on the Admin screen).
colListWhatever the list pop-up is showing (a day, or a dashboard card).
varRole, varThresh, varLabThe role, the warning days (60), and the wording of the three states.

Things deliberately kept simple

  • The refresh block is repeated in several handlers, generated from one source so the copies cannot drift. In a real build it would become a single App formula.
  • The type dropdown in the form lists every active type, not just those for the chosen programme.
  • The form shows one error message at a time.
  • "Who is attending" is plain text. A real build would use a people picker.
  • Search runs when you leave the box (modern text input fires on blur), not on every keystroke.

Source files

The generator and the files are in C:\Users\Phaderon\Projects\PowerApps-Apps\foe-calendar. Run python tools\gen_screens.py to rebuild, then bash tools\validate_all.sh and python tools\check_formulas.py to check.

Validation done before this page went live: Microsoft's YAML parser passes all five files, the layout check is clean, and my formula checks (brackets, quotes, every collection and variable defined) are clean. None of this has been run in Studio yet. That is what this test is for.
